# Commands · P · part 3

1,400 entries, pdk-skill-improver to pharn-memory-promote. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pdk-skill-improver**: Iteratively reviews and fixes a Claude Code skill until it meets quality standards. Triggers on 'fix my skill', 'improve skill quality', 'skill improvement loop'. ([AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace](https://github.com/AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pdk-skill-improver.md) · 2 copies)

- **pdk-update-dev-rule**: Find a stale plugin-devkit rule, update it and every other rule it affects using the official docs recommendation, then check for and fix any resulting staleness. ([AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace](https://github.com/AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pdk-update-dev-rule.md) · 2 copies)
- **pdk-verify-dev-rules**: Verify a dev-rules report against current official Claude Code documentation, identify gaps between local plugin-devkit rules and the platform, and re-verify every reported gap before finalizing a gap report. ([AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace](https://github.com/AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pdk-verify-dev-rules.md) · 2 copies)
- **pdk-verify-permissions**: Classify candidate permissions from pdk-find-permissions, and audit every existing settings.local.json/settings.json entry, for risky or destructive commands that belong in ask or deny instead of allow. ([AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace](https://github.com/AndreHahm/andres-cc-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pdk-verify-permissions.md) · 2 copies)

- **perma-consolidate**: Read-only consolidation pass over Permanence — analyse every stream and write a report of proposed changes. - **perma-consolidate-review**: Interactive walkthrough of a Permanence consolidation report — decide each item one at a time (yes / no / with-notes), see a final summary to catch mistakes + dependencies, then apply the accepted ones on `main` via a single audit branch. - **perma-contents**: Regenerate Permanence's `CONTENTS.md` inventory files. Argument: `$ARGUMENTS` — `local` (default), `full`, or a stream path like `home/kitchen`. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-contents.md) · ★1)
- **perma-emit**: Emit a Permanence event from THIS project to other open sessions — a semi-real-time note that something interesting happened here, delivered into other projects' context (never their files). - **perma-help**: What this Permanence can do — every installed /perma-* command, and which background machinery is actually switched on for this machine. Trigger phrases: "what can Permanence do", "Permanence help". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-help.md) · ★1)
- **perma-list**: List every registered Permanence stream — name, what it is, when it was last touched, and its real-world path if it has one. Read-only. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-list.md) · ★1)
- **perma-orchestrate**: Generative orchestration pass over Permanence — detect cross-stream convergence, entangle, and propose emergent ideas into the _meta/emergent.md ledger. - **perma-register**: Register an existing project as a Permanence stream — read its README, create the stream (seeded from the README), and add it to the registry. Use when the owner says "register this project", "register `&lt;path&gt;`", or passes a path to a README. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-register.md) · ★1)
- **perma-register-group**: Register a programme group — several repos that report into one shared plan-and-status folder. ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-register-group.md) · ★1)
- **perma-shutdown**: End-of-day wind-down — capture where things stand into this project's Permanence stream (PROJECT + QUESTIONS) and hand back a Done / in-flight / first-move-tomorrow summary, so the open loops leave your head instead of coming home with you. Optional argument: a Permanence stream/project name, OR an absolute path to a registered project — either works, for sessions with no meaningful working folder (desktop AI apps). Trigger phrases: "good night Permanence", "night Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-shutdown.md) · ★1)
- **perma-startup**: Morning bookend to /perma-shutdown — project-specific "where you left off" plus a small (2-3) set of next-task options for the current repo's Permanence stream. Optional argument: a Permanence stream/project name, OR an absolute path to a registered project — either works, for sessions with no meaningful working folder (desktop AI apps) or to switch projects mid-session. Trigger phrases: "good morning Permanence", "morning Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-startup.md) · ★1)
- **perma-unregister**: Remove a registered Permanence stream — deletes its folder (including gitignored derived files like CONTENTS.md) and its _meta/REGISTRY.md row in one step, so nothing is left orphaned. Destructive; always confirms first. Use when the owner says "unregister this project", "remove the &lt;name&gt; stream", or "delete the &lt;name&gt; project from Permanence". ([lotusboy/permanence](https://github.com/lotusboy/permanence/blob/HEAD/runtime/commands/perma-unregister.md) · ★1)

- **pfd-analyze**: Analyze a hypothetical change, mechanism, or behavioral pattern with Perception-First Design. Mode 3 descriptive analysis. Use for "what happens if", "why does X work", "users say X but do Y" questions that want results, not recommendations. ([jmorganthall/M365TCO](https://github.com/jmorganthall/M365TCO/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-analyze.md))

([jmorganthall/M365TCO](https://github.com/jmorganthall/M365TCO/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pfd-evaluate.md))
